\displaystyle\limsup_{\epsilon\to 0^{+}}\int_{C_{\rho}(0)}\bigg(\frac{3}{\epsilon}\int_{(-\frac{\rho}{2},\frac{\rho}{2})\times(-\infty,-\frac{\rho}{2})}K^{z}_{\epsilon}(t^{\prime}-t)|\overline{u}(y+t^{\prime}\xi)-z_{1}(0)|^{2}\,dt^{\prime}dt\bigg)dy

(8.34)

\displaystyle\leq C^{\prime\prime}\|\hat{K}^{z}\|_{L^{1}}\int_{C_{\rho}(0)}|u(y-\frac{\rho}{2}\xi)-z_{1}(0)|\,dy,

and by using that
\int_{C_{\rho}(0)}|\overline{z}_{1}(\frac{\epsilon y}{t}+\pi_{\xi}(z))-z_{1}(0)|^{2}\,dy=:f_{\epsilon}(t)\to\rho^{n-1}|\overline{z}_{1}(\pi_{\xi}(z))-z_{1}(0)|^{2}
uniformly in
t\in(-\infty,-\rho/2)
as
\epsilon\to 0^{+}
, again the very same technique gives

(8.35)

\displaystyle\limsup_{\epsilon\to 0^{+}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{C_{\rho}(0)}\bigg(

\displaystyle\frac{3}{\epsilon}\int_{(-\frac{\rho}{2},\frac{\rho}{2})\times(-\infty,-\frac{\rho}{2})}K^{z}_{\epsilon}(t^{\prime}-t)|\overline{z}_{1}(\frac{\epsilon y}{t}+\pi_{\xi}(z))-z_{1}(0)|^{2}\,dt^{\prime}dt\bigg)dy

\displaystyle\leq C^{\prime\prime}\|\hat{K}^{z}\|_{L^{1}}|\overline{z}_{1}(\pi_{\xi}(z))-z_{1}(0)|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C^{\prime\prime}\|\hat{K}^{z}\|_{L^{1}}\lfloor z_{1}\rfloor_{C^{0,\alpha}}^{2}\rho^{2\alpha}.

By combining (
8.33
)–(
8.35
), we infer that for a.e.
\rho>0
(sufficiently small)

\displaystyle\limsup_{\epsilon\to 0^{+}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{C_{\rho}(0)}\bigg(\int_{(-\frac{\rho}{2\epsilon},\frac{\rho}{2\epsilon})\times(-\infty,-\frac{\rho}{2\epsilon})}K^{z}(t^{\prime}-t)|\mathring{v}^{yz}_{\epsilon,\xi}(t^{\prime})-\mathring{v}^{yz}_{\epsilon,\xi}(t)|^{2}\,dt^{\prime}dt\bigg)dy

(8.36)

\displaystyle\leq C\|\hat{K}^{z}\|_{L^{1}}\bigg(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{C_{\rho}(0)}|u(y-\frac{\rho}{2}\xi)-z_{1}(0)|\,dy+\rho^{2\alpha}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bigg).

Analogously we obtain that for a.e.
\rho>0
(sufficiently small)

\displaystyle\limsup_{\epsilon\to 0^{+}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{C_{\rho}(0)}\bigg(\int_{(\frac{\rho}{2\epsilon},\frac{\rho}{2\epsilon})\times(\frac{\rho}{2\epsilon},\infty)}K^{z}(t^{\prime}-t)|\mathring{v}^{yz}_{\epsilon,\xi}(t^{\prime})-\mathring{v}^{yz}_{\epsilon,\xi}(t)|^{2}\,dt^{\prime}dt\bigg)dy

(8.37)

\displaystyle\leq C\|\hat{K}^{z}\|_{L^{1}}\bigg(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{C_{\rho}(0)}|u(y+\frac{\rho}{2}\xi)-z_{2}(0)|\,dy+\rho^{2\alpha}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bigg).

To conclude, properties (
8.31
) (
8.36
)–(
8.37
) yield (
8.30
).
∎

In the next lemma, we provide a fundamental estimate for the
\Gamma
-
\liminf
inequality.

Lemma 8.11

(The double liminf inequality)
.

Let
x_{0}\in\Omega
, let
(u_{\epsilon})_{\epsilon}
be a family of measurable functions
u_{\epsilon}\colon\Omega\to[-M,M]
. Assume that

(8.38)

u_{\epsilon}\to u,\ \ \text{ in $L^{1}(\Omega)$ as $\epsilon\to 0^{+}$}.

Then, by letting
v_{\epsilon}\colon S_{\rho}^{\epsilon}\to[-M,M]
be defined as
v_{\epsilon}(y+t\xi):=u_{\epsilon}(x_{0}+y+\epsilon t\xi)
, and by letting
I_{x_{0}}
be the set of
\rho>0
satisfying Proposition
8.10
, we have for every infinitesimal sequence
(\rho_{\ell})\subset I_{x_{0}}

(8.39)

\displaystyle\liminf_{\rho_{\ell}\to 0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\liminf_{\epsilon\to 0^{+}}F_{\epsilon,x_{0}}(\mathring{v}_{\epsilon,\xi};

\displaystyle S_{\rho_{\ell}}^{\epsilon})\rho_{\ell}^{1-n}\geq\inf_{\gamma\in X_{z_{1}(x_{0})}^{z_{2}(x_{0})}}F^{\xi}_{x_{0}}(\gamma)

(8.40)

\displaystyle-C\|\hat{J}\|_{L^{1}}\limsup_{\rho_{\ell}\to 0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\partial Q^{-}_{\rho_{\ell}}(x_{0})}|u(x)-z_{1}(x_{0})|\,d\mathcal{H}^{n-1}

(8.41)

\displaystyle-C\|\hat{J}\|_{L^{1}}\limsup_{\rho_{\ell}\to 0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\partial Q^{+}_{\rho_{\ell}}(x_{0})}|u(x)-z_{2}(x_{0})|\,d\mathcal{H}^{n-1},

where
C:=C(M,Z,\lfloor z_{1}\rfloor_{C^{0,\alpha}},\lfloor z_{2}\rfloor_{C^{0,\alpha}})>0
,
\partial Q_{\rho}(x_{0})^{\pm}
are defined in Proposition
8.10
, and
F^{\xi}_{x_{0}}
is given by (
2.5
).

Proof.

In order to simplify the notation we write
\rho
in place of
\rho_{\ell}
. Without loss of generality, we may assume that for every
\rho>0
small enough we have
\liminf_{\epsilon\to 0^{+}}F_{\epsilon,x_{0}}(\mathring{v}_{\epsilon,\xi};S_{\rho}^{\epsilon})<\infty
.
In what follows, we simplify the notation by assuming
x_{0}=0
.

We claim that

(8.42)

\begin{split}\liminf_{\rho\to 0}\liminf_{\epsilon\to 0^{+}}F_{\epsilon,0}(\mathring{v}_{\epsilon,\xi};S_{\rho}^{\epsilon})&\rho^{1-n}\geq\liminf_{\rho\to 0}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{C_{\rho}(0)}\mathcal{F}^{\rho\xi}_{y}(\gamma_{y,\rho})\,dy\\
&-C\|\hat{J}\|_{L^{1}}\limsup_{\rho\to 0}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\partial Q^{-}_{\rho}}|u(x)-z_{1}(0)|\,d\mathcal{H}^{n-1}\\
&-C\|\hat{J}\|_{L^{1}}\limsup_{\rho\to 0}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\partial Q^{+}_{\rho}}|u(x)-z_{2}(0)|\,d\mathcal{H}^{n-1}\end{split}

where
\gamma_{y,\rho}
denotes any increasing minimizer of
\mathcal{F}^{\rho\xi}_{y}
in the class
X^{b_{\rho}(y)}_{a_{\rho}(y)}
provided by Proposition
3.1
.

To prove the claim, we first notice that, since
\mathring{v}_{\epsilon,\xi}
is
C_{\rho}(0)
-periodic, then (
8.10
) entails that for every
\rho>0
small enough, and for every
\epsilon>0
there holds

(8.43)

F_{\epsilon,0}(\mathring{v}_{\epsilon,\xi};S_{\rho}^{\xi})\geq\int_{V_{\xi}}\bigg(\int_{C_{\rho}(0)}G^{yz}_{\rho,\epsilon}(\mathring{v}_{\epsilon,\xi}^{yz})\,dy\bigg)dz.

By (
8.20
) and (
8.30
), we have for
\mathcal{H}^{1}
-a.e. sufficiently small
\rho>0
and for
\mathcal{H}^{n-1}
-a.e.
z\in V_{\xi}

\displaystyle\liminf_{\epsilon\to 0^{+}}\int_{C_{\rho}(0)}G^{yz}_{\rho,\epsilon}(\mathring{v}_{\epsilon,\xi}^{yz})\,dy\geq\liminf_{\epsilon\to 0^{+}}\int_{C_{\rho}(0)}G^{yz}_{\rho}(\hat{v}_{\epsilon,z}^{yz})\,dy-C\rho^{\alpha+n-1}(\|\hat{K}^{z}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R})}+\rho^{\alpha}\|K^{z}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R})})

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt-c\|\hat{K}^{z}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R})}\int_{\partial Q^{-}_{\rho}}|u(x)-z_{1}(0)|\,d\mathcal{H}^{n-1}-c\|\hat{K}^{z}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R})}\int_{\partial Q^{+}_{\rho}}|u(x)-z_{2}(0)|\,d\mathcal{H}^{n-1},

where we also used that for every
z\in V_{\xi}
and
y\in V

\displaystyle\hat{v}_{\epsilon,z}^{yz}(t)

\displaystyle=\overline{a}_{\rho}(y)\ \ \text{ for }t\in(-\infty,-\rho/2\epsilon)

\displaystyle\hat{v}_{\epsilon,z}^{yz}(t)

\displaystyle=\overline{b}_{\rho}(y)\ \ \text{ for }t\in(\rho/2\epsilon,\infty)

and that for every
z\in V_{\xi}
and
y\in V

P^{z}_{\rho}(y,\overline{b}_{\rho}(y))=P^{z}_{\rho}(y,\overline{a}_{\rho}(y))=0.

By noticing that
t\mapsto\hat{v}_{\epsilon,z}^{yz}(t)
is admissible in the minimization of the functional
G^{yz}_{\rho}
among the class
X^{b_{\rho}(y)}_{a_{\rho}(y)}
for every
z\in V_{\xi}
and
y\in C_{\rho}(0)
, we find

\displaystyle\liminf_{\epsilon\to 0^{+}}\int_{C_{\rho}(0)}G^{yz}_{\rho,\epsilon}(\mathring{v}_{\epsilon,\xi}^{yz})\,dy\geq\int_{C_{\rho}(0)}G^{yz}_{\rho}(\gamma_{y,\rho})\,dy-C\rho^{\alpha+n-1}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\|\hat{K}^{z}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R})}+\rho^{\alpha}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\|K^{z}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R})})

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt-C\|\hat{K}^{z}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R})}\int_{\partial Q^{-}_{\rho}}|u(x)-z_{1}(0)|\,d\mathcal{H}^{n-1}

\displaystyle-C\|\hat{K}^{z}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R})}\int_{\partial Q^{+}_{\rho}}|u(x)-z_{2}(0)|\,d\mathcal{H}^{n-1}.

Hence by taking the integral with respect to
z\in V_{\xi}
on both sides of the previous inequality and using Fatou’s Lemma together with (
8.15
) we obtain for every
\rho>0
sufficiently small

\displaystyle\liminf_{\epsilon\to 0^{+}}\int_{V_{\xi}}\bigg(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{C_{\rho}(0)}

\displaystyle G^{yz}_{\rho,\epsilon}(\mathring{v}_{\epsilon,\xi}^{yz})\,dy\bigg)dz\geq\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{C_{\rho}(0)}\mathcal{F}^{\rho\xi}_{y}(\gamma_{y,\rho})\,dy

\displaystyle-C\rho^{\alpha}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bigg(\int_{V_{\xi}}\|\hat{K}^{z}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R})}\,dz+\rho^{\alpha}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\int_{V_{\xi}}\|K^{z}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R})}\,dz\bigg)

\displaystyle-C\bigg(\int_{V_{\xi}}\|\hat{K}^{z}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R})}\,dz\bigg)\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\partial Q^{-}_{\rho}}|u(x)-z_{1}(0)|\,d\mathcal{H}^{n-1}

\displaystyle-C\bigg(\int_{V_{\xi}}\|\hat{K}^{z}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R})}\,dz\bigg)\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\partial Q^{+}_{\rho}}|u(x)-z_{2}(0)|\,d\mathcal{H}^{n-1}.

Now we first notice that
\int_{V_{\xi}}\|\hat{K}^{z}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R})}\,dz=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}J(h)|h\cdot\xi|\,dh
and
\int_{V_{\xi}}\|K^{z}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R})}\,dz=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{m}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}J(h)\,dh
. Hence, (
8.42
) follows by taking the liminf as
\rho\to 0
on both sides of the above inequality in combination with (
8.43
).

The proposition thus follows if we show that

(8.44)

\liminf_{\rho\to 0}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{C_{\rho}(0)}\mathcal{F}^{\rho\xi}_{y}(\gamma_{y,\rho})\,dy\geq\inf_{\gamma\in X_{z_{2}(0)}^{z_{1}(0)}}F^{\xi}_{0}(\gamma).

A dilation in
y
by a factor
\rho
gives

(8.45)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{C_{\rho}(0)}\mathcal{F}^{\rho\xi}_{y}(\gamma_{y,\rho})\,dy

\displaystyle=\int_{C_{1}(0)}\bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}}J^{\xi}(h)|\gamma_{\rho y,\rho}(t+h)-\gamma_{\rho y,\rho}(t)|^{2}\,dtdh\bigg)dy

\displaystyle+\int_{C_{1}(0)}\bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathcal{W}^{\rho\xi}(\rho y,\gamma_{\rho y,\rho}(t))\,dt\bigg)dy.

By virtue of
(Y.1)
–
(Y.4)
we are in position to apply Proposition
3.13
with
a_{\rho},b_{\rho},W_{\rho},W_{0},\gamma_{\rho}
replaced by
a_{\rho}(\rho y),b_{\rho}(\rho y),\mathcal{W}^{\rho\xi}_{y}(\rho y,\cdot),W(0,\cdot),\gamma_{\rho y,\rho}
, respectively, and infer for every
y\in C_{\rho}(0)
that

(8.46)

\lim_{\rho\to 0}\int_{\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}}J^{\xi}(h)|\gamma_{\rho y,\rho}(t+h)-\gamma_{\rho y,\rho}(t)|^{2}\,dtdh+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathcal{W}^{\rho\xi}(\rho y,\gamma_{\rho y,\rho}(t))\,dt=F^{\xi}_{0}(\gamma_{0}),

where
\gamma_{0}
is any increasing minimizer of
F^{\xi}_{0}
in the class
X_{z_{1}(0)}^{z_{2}(0)}
(recall that
a_{\rho}(\rho y)\to z_{1}(0)
and
b_{\rho}(\rho y)\to z_{2}(0)
for every
y\in C_{1}(0)
). Therefore, we conclude the proof by applying Fatou’s lemma in (
8.45
).
∎

We are finally in a position to prove Proposition
7.2
.

Proof of Proposition
7.2
.

The statement follows by by letting
I_{x_{0}}
be the set of
\rho>0
satisfying Proposition
8.10
, putting together Lemma
8.11
and Remark
8.6
, as well as by observing that the result of Lemma
8.11
is invariant under reparametrizations of the kernel
J
in the
y
-variable.
∎
